## MOD_PLANET_RESETTLEMENT_UNEMPLOYED_DESTINATION_MULT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMOD_PLANET_RESETTLEMENT_UNEMPLOYED_DESTINATION_MULT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这一数值提高你帝国内高失业£pop£人口自发迁移向这一殖民地的概率，它由空余的岗位、住房和更佳的宜居性产生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

This value increases the chance unemployed £pop£ Pops within your empire will spontaneously migrate to this particular colony, so long as it has jobs, housing, and decent habitability.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:266`

## MOD_PLANET_RESETTLEMENT_UNEMPLOYED_MULT_DESC

### 中文

这个将会增加你的失业£pop£人口自动的移民到其他有空余岗位、住房并且是适合该人口居住的殖民地的概率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

This value increases the chance unemployed £pop£ Pops will spontaneously migrate to other colonies within your empire with jobs, housing, and decent habitability.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:268`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§E§H$MOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD$§!决定了帝国能有效补给的舰船数量。大型舰船比小型舰船要求更多的§H$MOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD$§!§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

§E§H$MOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD$§! determines the amount of ships that an empire can effectively supply. Larger ships require a higher §H$MOD_COUNTRY_NAVAL_CAP_ADD$§! than smaller ships.§!

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:560`

## tm_semi_static_we_defensive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atm_semi_static_we_defensive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

依照我们的§E半静态§!战争经济政策，我们英勇的公民开始做出牺牲，以顽强抵抗异星侵略者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1166`

### English

In accordance with our §ESemi-Static§! War Economy Policy, our valiant citizens have started to make sacrifices in our desperate defense against the alien aggressors.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1244`

## tm_semi_static_we_offensive_desc

### English

## capital

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acapital&lang=en)

### 中文

首都星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1378`

### English

Capital World

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1477`

## capital_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acapital_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星际帝国的首都星球上面充斥着政府建筑、办公室及代理服务机构的高楼大厦。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1379`

### English

The Capital planet of an interstellar empire is a vast complex of government buildings, bureaus and agency headquarters.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1478`

## distance_from_capital

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adistance_from_capital&lang=en)

### 中文

远离首都

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1380`

### English

Distance from Capital

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1479`

## managed_by_sector

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amanaged_by_sector&lang=en)

### 中文

受星域管辖

### English

Managed by Sector

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1480`

## hazardous_weather

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahazardous_weather&lang=en)

### 中文

变幻莫测的天气

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1382`

### English

Hazardous Weather

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1481`

## hazardous_weather_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahazardous_weather_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球上充斥着难以估计的天气变化，经常突然地出现猛烈的风暴，在地表造成浩劫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1383`

### English

This world suffers from unstable weather patterns, with violent storms frequently appearing out of nowhere to wreak havoc on the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1482`

## dangerous_wildlife

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adangerous_wildlife&lang=en)

### 中文

闹事的动物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1384`

### English

Hostile Fauna

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1483`

## dangerous_wildlife_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adangerous_wildlife_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球演化出了极度残暴的动物群，星球表面满是随时准备伏击他们下一顿餐食的致命捕食者。这些生物的生理学也提供了绝佳的研究机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1385`

### English

This world has evolved extremely Hostile Fauna, and the surface is rife with lethal predators waiting to ambush their next meal. Studying the creature's biology offers excellent research opportunities.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1484`

## weak_magnetic_field

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aweak_magnetic_field&lang=en)

### 中文

弱磁场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1386`

### English

Weak Magnetic Field

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1485`

## weak_magnetic_field_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aweak_magnetic_field_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管可以居住，但这个星球上微乎其微的磁场几乎无法抵御宇宙辐射。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1387`

### English

Although habitable, this world has a weak magnetic field that offers little protection against cosmic radiation.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1486`

## unstable_tectonics

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunstable_tectonics&lang=en)

### 中文

不稳定的构造

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1388`

### English

Unstable Tectonics

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1487`

## unstable_tectonics_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunstable_tectonics_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球拥有非常不稳定的板块构造，容易出现大规模地震和火山爆发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1389`

### English

This world has very unstable plate tectonics and is prone to massive earthquakes and volcanic activity.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1488`

## tidal_locked

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atidal_locked&lang=en)

### 中文

潮汐锁定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1390`

### English

Tidal Locked

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1489`

## tidal_locked_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atidal_locked_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球被其环绕的恒星锁定，这代表着它将不能进行自转，星球的两边分别是永恒的光明与永恒的黑暗，如果这个世界真的存在着生物，那么就只能出现在两边的交界线上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1391`

### English

This world is tidally locked around its star, meaning that one side always has daylight while the other is locked in eternal darkness. If life exists here, it is likely limited to the twilight regions where the two sides meet.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1490`

## chthonian_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Achthonian_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

气殒星核

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1392`

### English

Chthonian Planet

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1491`

## chthonian_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Achthonian_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球曾是一个气态巨行星，但因为过于接近恒星，其大气层被烧毁殆尽，只留下了一个密度密度，矿产丰富的星核。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1393`

### English

This planet was once a gas giant, but its proximity to its star has burned away the thick atmosphere. All that remains now is a super-dense and mineral rich metallic core.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1492`

## brown_dwarf

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abrown_dwarf&lang=en)

### 中文

褐矮星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1394`

### English

Brown Dwarf

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1493`

## brown_dwarf_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abrown_dwarf_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个气态巨行星实际上是一颗褐矮星——失败的恒星，缺乏必要的质量来维持氢核聚变。其特殊性质使得它拥有很大的研究价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1395`

### English

This gas giant is actually a brown dwarf - a failed star that lacked the necessary mass to sustain hydrogen fusion. Its special properties makes it valuable for research.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1494`

## asteroid_impacts

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_impacts&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星撞击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1396`

### English

Asteroid Impacts

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1495`

## asteroid_impacts_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_impacts_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球的轨道上散落着数目繁多的小行星，流星和彗星群，并经常撞击其表面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1397`

### English

The orbital path this world takes around its star is littered with small asteroids, meteors, and comet swarms. Large impacts on the surface occur at an alarming frequency.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1496`

## extensive_moon_system

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextensive_moon_system&lang=en)

### 中文

卫星繁多

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1398`

### English

Extensive Moon System

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1497`

## extensive_moon_system_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextensive_moon_system_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个气态巨行星拥有非比寻常多的天然卫星，轨道上充斥着数以百计的小卫星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1399`

### English

This gas giant has an unusually large amount of natural satellites. Hundreds of small moons and moonlets orbit the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1498`

## carbon_world

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acarbon_world&lang=en)

### 中文

碳星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1400`

### English

Carbon World

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1499`

## carbon_world_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acarbon_world_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球大部分由碳组成，含氧量极少。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1401`

### English

This world has been formed mostly out of carbon, and contains little oxygen.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1500`

## asteroid_coprolite

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_coprolite&lang=en)

### 中文

粪化石小行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1402`

### English

Coprolite Asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1501`

## asteroid_coprolite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_coprolite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗小行星实际上是一个巨大的粪化石——某种巨大生命的古老化石排泄物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1403`

### English

This asteroid is actually a gigantic coprolite - an ancient piece of fossilized feces from some kind of massive lifeform.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1502`

## asteroid_life

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_life&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星上的生命

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1404`

### English

Asteroid Life Forms

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1503`

## asteroid_life_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_life_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个小行星是一个大型石化蠕虫的洞穴家园，我们可以通过研究这一独特的生命模式获得许多有价值的资讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1405`

### English

This asteroid is home to a colony of large worm-like lithovores that burrow through the rock. Much could be learned by studying these unique life forms.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1504`

## derelict_shipyard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aderelict_shipyard&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃船厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1406`

### English

Derelict Shipyard

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1505`

## derelict_shipyard_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aderelict_shipyard_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾经有一个大型船厂在这个小行星内，珍贵的文物已经不知所踪，但遗留下来的建筑依然是工程界的奇迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1407`

### English

A large shipyard was built in the interior of this asteroid eons ago. It has been stripped of valuable artifacts, but remains a marvel of engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1506`

## junkyard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajunkyard&lang=en)

### 中文

垃圾场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1408`

### English

Junkyard

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1507`

## junkyard_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajunkyard_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个小行星曾是一个垃圾场，其表面遍布金属残骸。当中仍有一些具打捞价值的废品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1409`

### English

This asteroid served as a junkyard at some point and the surface is covered in metallic debris. Some of it can still be salvaged.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1508`

## target_range

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atarget_range&lang=en)

### 中文

武器标靶

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1410`

### English

Weapons Target Range

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1509`

## target_range_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atarget_range_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个可怜的小行星曾经被用作为亚空间武器测试目标物，其表面依然有异常的能量反应留存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1411`

### English

Someone used this poor asteroid as a target to test subspace weapons among other things. Anomalous energy echoes still linger on the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1510`

## ore_freighter

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aore_freighter&lang=en)

### 中文

坠毁的矿石货船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1412`

### English

Crashed Ore Freighter

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1511`

## ore_freighter_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aore_freighter_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

数千年前曾有一艘巨型矿石运输船在这个小行星上坠毁，货舱里的物品现在依然散落在小行星表面上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1413`

### English

A massive superfreighter carrying ore crashed on this asteroid thousands of years ago, and the contents of its cargo bays can still be found scattered on the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1512`

## atmospheric_ecosystem

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aatmospheric_ecosystem&lang=en)

### 中文

大气层生态系统

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1414`

### English

Atmospheric Ecosystem

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1513`

## atmospheric_ecosystem_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aatmospheric_ecosystem_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个气态巨行星的上方大气层是重要的生态系统，许多外星生物在此栖居。这种有趣的生物体系值得我们去进行研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1415`

### English

The upper atmosphere of this gas giant features a significant ecosystem that is home to many strange alien life forms. Their interesting biology is a promising research subject.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1514`

## aerostat_colonies

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaerostat_colonies&lang=en)

### 中文

浮空殖民地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1416`

### English

Aerostat Colonies

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1515`

## aerostat_colonies_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaerostat_colonies_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个气态巨行星的低轨道上仍然能找到古代的浮空殖民地的废墟。其异常坚固的设计是先进工程的产物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1417`

### English

A crumbling network of ancient aerostat colonies can still be found in low orbit of this gas giant. Their unusually sturdy design is the product of advanced engineering.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1516`

## debris_field

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adebris_field&lang=en)

### 中文

残骸场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1418`

### English

Debris Field

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1517`

## debris_field_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adebris_field_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一堆太空残骸被这个巨型气态行星的强大重力井所捕获，虽然大部份都是些废品，但内含的金属可以进行回收加以运用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1419`

### English

A dense layer of space debris has been captured by the strong gravity well of this gas giant. Most of it is worthless, but the metal can be recycled.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1518`

## extreme_storms

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_storms&lang=en)

### 中文

究极风暴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1420`

### English

Extreme Storms

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1519`

## extreme_storms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aextreme_storms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

数之不尽的巨型风暴在这个气态巨行星上肆虐，我们无法透过气候模型来解释这个情况，显然这是一个有价值的研究对像。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1421`

### English

Dozens of massive storms are raging in the atmosphere of this gas giant. Climate models have failed to explain their presence, making the planet an interesting subject of research.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1520`

## precious_moon

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprecious_moon&lang=en)

### 中文

珍宝卫星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1422`

### English

Precious Moon

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1521`

## precious_moon_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprecious_moon_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

环绕这颗气态巨行星的庞大天然卫星系统中有一颗富含贵重金属和矿物的小卫星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1423`

### English

A small moonlet largely consisting of precious metals and minerals can be found within the extensive system of natural satellites that orbit this gas giant.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1522`

## asteroid_collision

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_collision&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星碰撞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1424`

### English

Asteroid Collision

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1523`

## asteroid_collision_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_collision_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不到一千年以前，一颗大质量的小行星撞击了这个星球，大量矿物沉积于此。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1425`

### English

A massive asteroid impacted this world less than a thousand years ago, depositing a large amount of minerals.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1524`

## microsingularity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amicrosingularity&lang=en)

### 中文

微型奇点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1426`

### English

Microsingularity

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1525`

## microsingularity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amicrosingularity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾有一个微型奇点干净俐落的穿过这个星球的核心，并留下了一些有趣的能量残留。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1427`

### English

A microsingularity passed clean through the core of this world, causing some interesting energy emissions.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1526`

## alien_writing

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_writing&lang=en)

### 中文

外星手迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1428`

### English

Alien Writing

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1527`

## alien_writing_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_writing_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有人曾利用采矿激光在这个世界表面刻上一些即使在轨道上仍能看到的文字。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1429`

### English

Someone has carved a large body of text into the surface of this world with a mining laser. The alien letters are large enough to be visible from orbit.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1528`

## towed_asteroid

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atowed_asteroid&lang=en)

### 中文

被牵引的小行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1430`

### English

Towed Asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1529`

## towed_asteroid_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atowed_asteroid_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有人将这颗巨大的小行星牵引到这个星球的稳定轨道上，或许是为了开采以获得资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1431`

### English

Someone has towed a large asteroid into a stable orbit around this world, presumably to mine it for resources.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1530`

## nuclear_devastation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anuclear_devastation&lang=en)

### 中文

核毁灭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1432`

### English

Nuclear Devastation

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1531`

## nuclear_devastation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anuclear_devastation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球曾经历过一次由土著发起的毁灭性世界大战，最终形成有毒的大气层，我们能够透过研究这些死寂废墟来获得有价值的研究资料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1433`

### English

The toxic atmosphere of this world is the result of a destructive global war fought between factions of an indigenous civilization. Valuable research can be done by studying the lifeless ruins.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1532`

## nuclear_wasteland

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anuclear_wasteland&lang=en)

### 中文

核废墟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1434`

### English

Nuclear Wasteland

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1533`

## nuclear_wasteland_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anuclear_wasteland_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在过去的某个时间点，这个星球曾经历过一次由土著发起的核战。其破坏的生物圈至今仍未恢复，辐射的阴影仍然存在笼罩着这个世界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1435`

### English

This world was subjected to a massive exchange of nuclear warheads between factions of an indigenous civilization at some point in the past. The biosphere has yet to fully recover and there are still lingering pockets of radiation.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1534`

## gamma_ray

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agamma_ray&lang=en)

### 中文

伽马线爆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1436`

### English

Gamma-Ray Burst

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1535`

## gamma_ray_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agamma_ray_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很久以前，这个星球处于恒星坍缩時引起的伽马射线曝晒下，它的臭氧层至今还没有完全恢复，這意味着所有殖民者都要采取额外的紫外线保护措施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1437`

### English

Long ago, this world was in the path of a gamma-ray burst from a collapsing star. The ozone layer has yet to fully recover, meaning that any potential colonists will need extra protection against UV radiation.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1536`

## greenhouse_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agreenhouse_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

失控温室效应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1438`

### English

Runaway Greenhouse Effect

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1537`

## greenhouse_effect_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agreenhouse_effect_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球受到当地处于工业时代的土著无节制产生的温室气体所污染，并引发了温室气体失控，一些古代工厂和居住地区的无人废墟在有毒的大气层下仍然清晰可见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1439`

### English

An indigenous industrial civilization polluted this world to the point where a runaway greenhouse effect was triggered. The lifeless ruins of ancient factories and population centers can still be found on the toxic surface.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1538`

## ammonia_biosphere

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aammonia_biosphere&lang=en)

### 中文

氨生物圈

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1440`

### English

Ammonia Biosphere

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1539`

## ammonia_biosphere_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aammonia_biosphere_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个有毒的星球拥有一种使用氨而非水份为溶剂的生物圈，研究这一种氨基生物将会对我们的生物学带来长足的发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1441`

### English

This toxic world is home to a rare ecology where life uses ammonia instead of water as a solvent. These ammonia-based life forms have developed in unique ways, and studying them would advance our understanding of biology.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1540`

## silicon_lifeforms

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asilicon_lifeforms&lang=en)

### 中文

硅基生命

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1442`

### English

Silicon Life Forms

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1541`

## silicon_lifeforms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asilicon_lifeforms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

穴居的半智能硅基生命形式生活在这个星球地表下的大型隧道网络中。它们时常能将挖掘到的贵重金属和矿物带到地表来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1443`

### English

Burrowing, semi-intelligent life forms made out of silicon inhabit a large tunnel network beneath the surface of this world. Their digging efforts frequently shuffles valuable metals and minerals to the surface.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1542`

## colony_ruins

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_ruins&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民地遗迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1444`

### English

Colony Ruins

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1543`

## colony_ruins_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_ruins_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球上有一个属于未知的星际帝国的古老殖民地遗迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1445`

### English

Ancient ruins from a small abandoned colony belonging to an unknown interstellar empire can be found on this world.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1544`

## alien_mural

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_mural&lang=en)

### 中文

外星壁画

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1446`

### English

Alien Mural

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1545`

## alien_mural_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalien_mural_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球表面上的一副古老壁画记载了一个早已消亡的外星文明掌握的技术知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1447`

### English

An ancient mural on the surface of this world recounts the technological knowledge of a long-dead alien civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1546`

## migrating_forests

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amigrating_forests&lang=en)

### 中文

迁徙森林

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1448`

### English

Migrating Forests

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1547`

## migrating_forests_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amigrating_forests_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个世界的迁徙森林频繁集体迁移到新的地区，破坏建设好的道路，输电线路和其他基础设施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1449`

### English

The mobile forests of this world frequently migrate en masse to new regions, disrupting efforts at building roads, power lines and other infrastructure.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1548`

## migrating_forests_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amigrating_forests_2&lang=en)

### 中文

温顺的迁徙森林

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1450`

### English

Docile Migrating Forests

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1549`

## migrating_forests_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amigrating_forests_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§W添加§Y迁徙森林保护区§!修正，提升该星球上的£society£ §Y社会学研究§!。§!\n\n该星球的迁徙森林现在会隔离在居民区之外，也不会再对殖民地造成损害了，但其存在仍然会限制殖民者可用的土地规模。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1451`

### English

§W§YMigrating Forest Reserve§! added, improving £society£ §YSociety Research§! on the planet.§!\n\nThe wandering forests on this world now avoid populated areas and no longer cause damage to settlements. Their presence still limits the amount of free land available to colonists.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1550`

## damaged_ecology

生态受损

Damaged Ecology

## damaged_ecology_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adamaged_ecology_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该星球的迁徙林木遭到人为破坏，将对生态造成显著影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1453`

### English

The destruction of the wandering forests on this world has had a significant effect on the ecology.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1552`

## friendly_trees

适应共存

### English

Forest Adaptation

## friendly_trees_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afriendly_trees_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球上的森林与殖民者互相接纳了对方，这使他们的精神振奋了一段时日。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1455`

### English

The forests and colonists on this world have adapted to each other, raising spirits for a time.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1554`

## subterranean_civilization

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubterranean_civilization&lang=en)

### 中文

地下文明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1456`

### English

Subterranean Civilization

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1555`

## subterranean_civilization_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubterranean_civilization_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球上的地下外星文明与地表上的殖民者拥有良好关系，双方经常从事贸易和文化交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1457`

### English

The subterranean alien civilization on this world enjoys a good relationship with the colonists on the surface, and both parties frequently engage in trade and cultural exchanges.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1556`

## subterranean_expansion

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubterranean_expansion&lang=en)

### 中文

地下城市

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1458`

### English

Subterranean Cities

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1557`

## subterranean_expansion_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubterranean_expansion_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾经生活在这个世界上的地下土著文明已经灭绝了，殖民者现在能够自由扩展到他们废弃的地下城市。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1459`

### English

The indigenous subterranean civilization that once lived on this world has been exterminated, and colonists are now free to expand into their abandoned underground cities.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1558`

## gas_giant_civ

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agas_giant_civ&lang=en)

### 中文

达斯纳克首都

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1460`

### English

Dathnak Capital

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1559`

## gas_giant_civ_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agas_giant_civ_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球是达斯纳克的首都和母星。这些气态生物的工程和科研技术十分落后，但我们可以从他们当中学到很多社会科学。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1461`

### English

This world is the new capital and adopted homeworld of the Dathnak. These sapient gaseous beings have no industry or technology, but they appear to be quite social.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1560`

## gas_giant_colony

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agas_giant_colony&lang=en)

### 中文

达斯纳克殖民地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1462`

### English

Dathnak Colony

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1561`

## gas_giant_colony_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agas_giant_colony_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被称为达斯纳克的智慧生命形式在这颗气态巨行星上建立了一个小型殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1463`

### English

The sapient gaseous life forms known as the Dathnak have established a small colony within the atmosphere of this gas giant.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1562`

## abandoned_terraforming

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aabandoned_terraforming&lang=en)

### 中文

废弃的环境改造计划

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1464`

### English

Abandoned Terraforming Project

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1563`

## abandoned_terraforming_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aabandoned_terraforming_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球在几千年前曾被试图进行地表改造，但基于不明原因，这一进程被打断，并导致了星球内的气候系统和生物圈极其不稳定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1465`

### English

This world was partly terraformed thousands of years ago, but the process was interrupted for unknown reasons. The result is a highly unstable climate system and biosphere.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1564`

## gaia_world

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agaia_world&lang=en)

### 中文

古代环境改造技术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1466`

### English

Ancient Terraforming

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1565`

## gaia_world_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Agaia_world_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

古代环境改造技术将这个星球改造为一个郁郁葱葱的天堂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1467`

### English

Ancient terraforming techniques have turned this world into a lush paradise.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1566`

## mutant_stalker

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutant_stalker&lang=en)

### 中文

掠杀怪物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1468`

### English

Marauding Creature

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1567`

## mutant_stalker_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amutant_stalker_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个突变的生命体正在猎杀这个星球上的殖民者，邻里乡间人心惶惶。我们的殖民地的发展受到影响，工人都不愿意冒险进入荒野。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1469`

### English

A mutated creature is hunting the colonists on this world, spreading terror in the countryside. The development of our colony has been impacted, as workers are reluctant to venture into the wilderness.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1568`

## wasteland_radiation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awasteland_radiation&lang=en)

### 中文

废土辐照

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1470`

### English

Wasteland Radiation

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1569`

## wasteland_radiation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awasteland_radiation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

数个定居点附近的大量辐照为当地殖民者带来严重的负面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1471`

### English

High levels of radiation near several settlements are having an adverse effect on the health of the colonists living there.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1570`

## wasteland_orbital_debris

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awasteland_orbital_debris&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道残骸

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1472`

### English

Orbital Debris

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1571`

## wasteland_orbital_debris_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awasteland_orbital_debris_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这个星球上的轨道上充斥着残骸。不仅无法送地表发射卫星，而且残骸密集区也令导航难以进行，这严重阻碍了殖民地的基础建设。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1473`

### English

The orbital space over this world is littered with debris. Satellites cannot be launched from the surface, and the dense field has become a navigational hazard. This is seriously hampering the development of the colony's infrastructure.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1572`

## wasteland_infrastructure

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awasteland_infrastructure&lang=en)

### 中文

幸存的基础设施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1474`

### English

Surviving Infrastructure

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1573`

## wasteland_infrastructure_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awasteland_infrastructure_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球的原居民在一场使用核武的战争中自我毁灭，但仍有大量的基础设施得以保留。这将为我们建立新设施提供帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1475`

### English

There is still a significant amount of infrastructure left on this world, built by its original inhabitants before they destroyed themselves in a nuclear war. This will aid in the construction of new buildings.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1574`

## forest_fire

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aforest_fire&lang=en)

### 中文

森林大火

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1476`

### English

Forest Fire

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1575`

## forest_fire_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aforest_fire_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

森林大火导致了迁徙林木的大规模踩踏事件，并对几个农耕区域造成暂时性损害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1477`

### English

A forest fire resulted in a stampede by this world's wandering forests, causing temporary damage to several farming regions.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1576`

### 中文

这里有可被提升的物种，假如我们在这个星球上建立殖民地，那么我们就不再会去提升它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1479`

### English

## abundant_geothermal_activity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aabundant_geothermal_activity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个行星有许多可供利用的地热区。尽管这带来了一些工程上的挑战，但这也给予了额外产出能源的机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1497`

### English

This planet has many accessible geothermal hot spots. While this poses some engineering challenges, it also provides extra energy generation opportunities.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1598`

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1599`

## subterranean_wildlife_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubterranean_wildlife_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个行星有一个繁荣的地下岩性生态系统。一群性情温和的噬矿地下生物挖出了大型洞穴，这也使得它们可用于采矿和殖民。这些动物也为旨在寻找大量矿藏的矿工提供了独特的研究对象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1499`

### English

This planet has a thriving underground lithotrophic ecosystem. Herds of mild mineral-crunching subterranean creatures dig out large caverns, leaving them available for mining and colony use. The animals provide unique research opportunities for the miners who direct the packs to valuable mineral deposits.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1600`

## rich_mircoflora

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arich_mircoflora&lang=en)

### 中文

丰富的菌群

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1500`

### English

Rich Microflora

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1601`

## rich_mircoflora_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arich_mircoflora_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球的土壤是一个活跃且高产的微生物生态系统的家园，这个微生物生态系统不仅提供丰富的农业产出，还能够作为研究对象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1501`

### English

The soil of this world is home to a robust and highly productive microflora ecosystem, allowing for rich farming and research opportunities.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1602`

## strong_magnetic_field

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astrong_magnetic_field&lang=en)

### 中文

强力磁场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1502`

### English

Strong Magnetic Field

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1603`

## strong_magnetic_field_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astrong_magnetic_field_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球有异常强大的磁场，对我们的各种仪器和计算机造成影响。缓解这些影响将会有助于我们推进力场操控研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1503`

### English

This planet has an abnormally high magnetic field and will interfere with many of our instruments and computers. Mitigating these effects will help inform field manipulation research.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1604`

## wild_storms

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awild_storms&lang=en)

### 中文

狂野之风

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1504`

### English

Wild Storms

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1605`

## wild_storms_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Awild_storms_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

激烈的电子流风暴以难以预料的频率不断侵袭着这个星球的大气层。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1505`

### English

Spectacular electrical storms wrack this planet's atmosphere on a semi-regular basis.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1606`

## low_gravity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alow_gravity&lang=en)

### 中文

低重力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1506`

### English

Low Gravity

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1607`

## low_gravity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alow_gravity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星有着异常低的密度，因此重力也比其他相同体积的星球低许多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1507`

### English

This planet has an unusually low density and therefore much lower gravity than most planets of comparable size.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1608`

## high_gravity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahigh_gravity&lang=en)

### 中文

高重力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1508`

### English

High Gravity

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1609`

## high_gravity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahigh_gravity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星有着异常高的密度，因此形成了令人难受的强重力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1509`

### English

This planet is unusually dense and so has uncomfortably high gravity.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1610`

## hollow_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahollow_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

中空行星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1510`

### English

Hollow Planet

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1611`

## hollow_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahollow_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星基于不明原因和手法被人为地或莫名其妙地掏空了，调查它将会是一项有趣的事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1511`

### English

This planet has either been artificially created or was somehow hollowed out. How or why we do not know, but investigating it promises to be fascinating.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1612`

## mineral_rich

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amineral_rich&lang=en)

### 中文

高品质矿物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1512`

### English

High Quality Minerals

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1613`

## mineral_rich_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amineral_rich_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星地壳蕴藏的丰富高品质矿藏使采矿在这里成为一项获利颇丰的事业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1513`

### English

An abundance of high quality minerals in this planet's crust makes mining here a lucrative business.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1614`

## titanic_life

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atitanic_life&lang=en)

### 中文

泰坦巨兽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1514`

### English

Titanic Life

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1615`

## titanic_life_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atitanic_life_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球的生命体有着异常庞大的体型，即便它们本身没有恶意，其存在也是相当危险的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1515`

### English

The lifeforms of this planet are built on a massive scale and can be incredibly dangerous even without meaning any harm.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1616`

## eat_the_titans

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeat_the_titans&lang=en)

### 中文

美味巨兽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1516`

### English

Delicious Titans

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1617`

## eat_the_titans_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeat_the_titans_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

出乎意料的，在收集这些巨兽用作科研的过程中，我们发现这些东西口感相当美味！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1517`

### English

Harvesting the Titanic life for research data has had an unexpected side benefit: They taste great!

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1618`

## eat_the_titans_hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeat_the_titans_hive&lang=en)

$eat_the_titans$

$eat_the_titans$

$eat_the_titans_desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1519`

### English

$eat_the_titans_desc$

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1620`

## eat_the_titan_evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeat_the_titan_evopred&lang=en)

$eat_the_titan$

$eat_the_titan$

## asteroid_belt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_belt&lang=en)

### 中文

小行星带

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1522`

### English

Asteroid Belt

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1623`

## asteroid_belt_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aasteroid_belt_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星有一个小行星环带。最有可能是其微小的卫星与其他天体碰撞之后留下的残骸，碎片场成为了一片富饶但具有工程复杂性的潜在采矿场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1523`

### English

This planet has a belt of minor asteroids orbiting around it. Most likely caused by a tiny moon destroyed in a collision with another planetary body, the debris field provides ample but engineeringly complex mining opportunities.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1624`

## natural_beauty

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anatural_beauty&lang=en)

### 中文

自然景观

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1524`

### English

Natural Beauty

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1625`

## natural_beauty_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Anatural_beauty_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星上的景色处处秀丽，行星上的居民总会持续不断地寻找到新的自然奇迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1525`

### English

The scenery on this planet is simply beautiful, anyone living on this planet will be constantly finding new wonders to behold.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1626`

## atmospheric_aphrodisiac

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aatmospheric_aphrodisiac&lang=en)

### 中文

催情大气

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1526`

### English

Atmospheric Aphrodisiac

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1627`

## atmospheric_aphrodisiac_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aatmospheric_aphrodisiac_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球的大气层中蕴含着的物质使当地人变得，唔，精力旺盛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1527`

### English

Something in the air of this planet seems to put people in the mood to reproduce.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1628`

## atmospheric_hallucinogen

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aatmospheric_hallucinogen&lang=en)

### 中文

致幻大气

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1528`

### English

Atmospheric Hallucinogen

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1629`

## atmospheric_hallucinogen_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aatmospheric_hallucinogen_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球的空气里似乎含有某种天然的致幻剂。它确实能使人心情愉悦，也似乎能让某些物理学之谜变得易解；可同时也会让人无法集中精力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1529`

### English

The air of this planet contains a natural hallucinogen. While this does keep people happy, and indeed seems to aid the visualization of some of the more esoteric Physics problems, it also distracts people from other work.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1630`

## atmospheric_hallucinogen_good

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aatmospheric_hallucinogen_good&lang=en)

### 中文

过滤的致幻大气

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1530`

### English

Filtered Atmospheric Hallucinogen

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1631`

## atmospheric_hallucinogen_good_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aatmospheric_hallucinogen_good_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球的空气里曾含有某种天然致幻剂。而如今经过过滤后致幻物质已被稀释。尽管少了一些原本的好处，但同时也消除了所有的负面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1531`

### English

The air of this planet once contained a natural hallucinogen. Now, with the aid of massive filters, the effect has been diluted. This has reduced the benefits it once gave, but has also eliminated the negatives.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1632`

## lush_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alush_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

郁郁葱葱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1532`

### English

Lush

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1633`

## lush_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Alush_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

生命在这个星球上蓬勃发展，动植物种类繁多，且生长迅速而繁盛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1533`

### English

Life thrives here, the fauna is plentiful and the flora grows rapidly.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1634`

## bleak_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ableak_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

苍茫大地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1534`

### English

Bleak

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1635`

## bleak_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ableak_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

生物难以在这里生存，尽管有些物种顽强地寓居于此，它们也都瘦小而虚弱。这些问题将会影响到粮食的生产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1535`

### English

Life struggles to survive here, and while some clings to a tenacious existence, it is frail and sickly. These issues will harm food production.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1636`

## irradiated_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airradiated_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

辐照天地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1536`

### English

Irradiated

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1637`

## irradiated_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Airradiated_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场很久以前的灾难让这颗星球笼罩在严重的辐射之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1537`

### English

Some long-ago calamity rendered this planet rather heavily irradiated.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1638`

## ancient_automation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_automation&lang=en)

### 中文

古代自动化技术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1538`

### English

Ancient Automation

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1639`

## ancient_automation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_automation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管历史悠久，但星球上的机器仍为我们的生产工作提供了良好的帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1539`

### English

Ancient, yet helpful, machines aid our production on this planet.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1640`

## ancient_factory

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_factory&lang=en)

### 中文

古代工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1540`

### English

Ancient Factory

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1641`

## ancient_factory_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_factory_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星上的一座古代工厂仍然可以运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1541`

### English

An ancient factory on this planet is still in operation condition.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1642`

## robot_workers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arobot_workers&lang=en)

### 中文

机器人劳工

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1542`

### English

Robot Workers

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1643`

## robot_workers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arobot_workers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由某个遗落文明所制造的机器人劳工仍留存在这颗行星上。在采取适当的防范措施后，这些机器人就能为我们效命了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1543`

### English

Robot workers from a forgotten civilization remain on this planet. With the proper precautions, they could be put to work for us.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1644`

## ancient_forgeworld

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_forgeworld&lang=en)

### 中文

古代铸造星球

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1544`

### English

Ancient Forgeworld

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1645`

## ancient_forgeworld_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_forgeworld_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星原本是某个遗落文明的工业制造中心。尽管只有一小部分仪器仍然能运行，但也为我们的工业作出了很好的示范。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1545`

### English

This planet was once some kind of center of production for a forgotten civilization. Although only a shadow of its former capacity remains in working condition, it is still an impressive example of industry.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1646`

## holy_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholy_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

圣地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1546`

### English

Holy World

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1647`

## holy_planet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aholy_planet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星被邻近的失落帝国视为圣地，假如我们在其之上殖民，将会大大的触怒失落帝国。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1547`

### English

This planet is considered sacred by a nearby Fallen Empire. Colonizing it will anger them greatly.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1648`

## ancient_weapon

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_weapon&lang=en)

### 中文

古代轨道武器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1548`

### English

Ancient Orbital Weapon

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1649`

## ancient_weapon_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aancient_weapon_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎某些遗落的物种在这个星球的轨道上建造了这个巨大的轨道武器，不过这个工程尚未完成，我们或许可以从中学习到新事物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1549`

### English

It seems that some long-forgotten race once build some kind of huge weapon in orbit above this world. While it doesn't work, indeed it doesn't appear to have been completed, we can likely learn a few things by studying it.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1650`

## terraforming_candidate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_candidate&lang=en)

### 中文

环境改造候选

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1550`

### English

Terraforming Candidate

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1651`

## terraforming_candidate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_candidate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管表面荒芜贫瘠毫无生机，这颗星球上仍存在有以冻结形态存在的水，这里也许曾经孕育过一个生机勃勃的生态系统。如果利用合适的科技加以环境改造，这里可能成为一个宜居的世界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1551`

### English

Though barren and devoid of life, this planet has water in frozen form and may once have been home to a thriving ecosystem. With the right technology, it could be terraformed into a habitable world.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1652`

## terraforming_candidate_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aterraforming_candidate_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

只要我们研究了£society£ §Y$tech_climate_restoration$§!科技，我们就能对这颗行星环境改造，将其转化为一个宜居星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1552`

### English

Once we have researched the £society£ §Y$tech_climate_restoration$§! technology, we will be able to terraform this planet into a habitable world.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1653`

## frozen_terraforming_candidate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afrozen_terraforming_candidate&lang=en)

### 中文

冰封环境改造候选

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1553`

### English

Frozen Terraforming Candidate

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1654`

## frozen_terraforming_candidate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afrozen_terraforming_candidate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管寒冷到无法支持生命，但只要我们找到合适的方法，便可以利用该星球上冻结的水将其改造成一颗宜居星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1554`

### English

Though cold to the point of being unable to sustain life, the frozen water on this world could allow us to terraform it into a habitable world, if we can find the right method.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1655`

## frozen_terraforming_candidate_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afrozen_terraforming_candidate_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们选择了§Y$ap_hydrocentric$§!飞升天赋并研究了£society£§Y$tech_climate_restoration$§!科技，我们就可以将这颗行星改造成一颗宜居性星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1555`

### English

If we pick the §Y$ap_hydrocentric$§! ascension perk and research the £society£ §Y$tech_climate_restoration$§! technology, we will be able to terraform this planet into a habitable world.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1656`

## toxic_terraforming_candidate

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxic_terraforming_candidate&lang=en)

### 中文

剧毒环境改造候选

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1556`

### English

Toxic Terraforming Candidate

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1657`

## toxic_terraforming_candidate_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxic_terraforming_candidate_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管该星球的剧毒大气使其无法支持生命，但通过合适的手段可以将其分解成不那么致命的物质，使其逐渐变得宜居。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1557`

### English

Though the noxious gases on this world make it unfit for life, with enough effort they can be broken down into less lethal compounds, eventually allowing colonization.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1658`

## toxic_terraforming_candidate_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atoxic_terraforming_candidate_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

如果我们选择了§Y$ap_detox$§!飞升天赋，我们就可以将这颗行星改造成一颗宜居性星球。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1558`

### English

If we pick the §Y$ap_detox$§! ascension perk, we will be able to terraform this planet into a habitable world.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1659`

## ultra_rich

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aultra_rich&lang=en)

### 中文

超品矿物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1559`

### English

Exceptional Quality Minerals

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1660`

## ultra_rich_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aultra_rich_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球简直就是一个宝库！超高品质的矿物无疑是一个惊人的利润来源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1560`

### English

The exceptional quality of the minerals in this planet's crust provides a truly staggering opportunity for mining.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1661`

## mineral_poor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amineral_poor&lang=en)

### 中文

低品矿物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1561`

### English

Poor Quality Minerals

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1662`

## mineral_poor_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amineral_poor_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

人们至今仍不清楚这颗行星究竟是生来如此，亦或是某种未知物种曾经到来将它的许多珍贵资源攫取一空。无论怎样，我们清楚的是这颗行星现在的矿物质量异常低劣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1562`

### English

It is unknown if this planet simply formed this way, or if some unknown race once stripped it of its more valuable resources. However, we do know that the minerals present on this planet are of unusually poor quality.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1663`

## planet_uncertain_past

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_uncertain_past&lang=en)

### 中文

模糊的过往

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1563`

### English

Uncertain history

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1664`

## planet_uncertain_past_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aplanet_uncertain_past_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球上的居民一致对许久之前发生的一场灾难耿耿于怀，现在正在试图找出真正的原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1564`

### English

The people of this planet are troubled by not knowing what caused the disaster which befell it long ago, and are driven to try to find out.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1665`

## recent_quake

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arecent_quake&lang=en)

### English

## recent_quake_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arecent_quake_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星近期一直受到强烈的地质运动影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1566`

### English

This planet was recently rocked by a violent Tectonic Event

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1667`

## recent_comet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arecent_comet&lang=en)

### 中文

发现彗星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1567`

### English

Comet sighted

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1668`

## recent_comet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arecent_comet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一颗彗星最近在这颗星球的小行星带中穿过，人们在地表上就能用肉眼看见这壮观的景象！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1568`

### English

A comet recently passed through this planet's Asteroid Belt, which was a spectacular sight!

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1669`

## recent_impact

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arecent_impact&lang=en)

### 中文

近期陨石撞击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1569`

### English

Recent Impact

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1670`

## recent_impact_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arecent_impact_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当地的殖民地最近被一个大型的小行星撞击所彻底摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1570`

### English

The local colony was recently rocked by the impact of a large Asteroid

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1671`

## magnetic_research_boost

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amagnetic_research_boost&lang=en)

### 中文

磁力奇迹

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1571`

### English

Magnetic Miracle

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1672`

## magnetic_research_boost_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amagnetic_research_boost_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不知何故，这个星球的磁场干扰了我们的电脑，但没有造成寻常的破坏，反而使电脑运作更高效！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1572`

### English

Somehow the Magnetic field of this planet is interfering with our computers, but rather than a simple disruption it is actually making them more efficient!

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1673`

## magnetic_disruption

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amagnetic_disruption&lang=en)

### 中文

磁力破坏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1573`

### English

Magnetic Disruption

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1674`

## magnetic_disruption_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amagnetic_disruption_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球的磁场干扰着我们的电脑设备，严重影响了研究工作，民众也为此烦恼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1574`

### English

The Magnetic field of this planet is interfering with our computers, greatly disrupting research work and upsetting the populace.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1675`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astone_age_civilization&lang=en)

Stone Age pre-FTLs
