## stone_age_civilization_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astone_age_civilization_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星孕育了一个尚处于石器时代的文明原型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1623`

### English

This world has produced a Stone Age proto-civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1728`

## dead_creatures

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adead_creatures&lang=en)

### 中文

太空墓地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1624`

### English

Dead Space Creatures

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1729`

## dead_creatures_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Adead_creatures_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个巨型气态行星是一个古代太空生物的墓地，我们能够研究它们的尸体来获得有价值的资讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1625`

### English

This gas giant served as an ancient graveyard for a species of spacefaring creatures. Many corpses can still be found in orbit, offering valuable research.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1730`

## starship_graveyard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astarship_graveyard&lang=en)

### 中文

星空舰塚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1626`

### English

Starship Graveyard

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1731`

## starship_graveyard_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astarship_graveyard_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球的轨道上曾经发生一场大型的战争，并在星球表面留下了许多古代的舰船残骸，研究这些残骸将会获得许多有用的知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1627`

### English

A great battle took place in orbit of this world millennia ago, and the surface is still littered with the wreckage of ancient starships. Studying their advanced designs will yield valuable knowledge.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1732`

## organic_starship

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorganic_starship&lang=en)

### 中文

有机星舰

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1628`

### English

Organic Starship

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1733`

## organic_starship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorganic_starship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

某种有机体舰船三十亿年前在这个星球上坠毁。虽然只有古老的骨头留了下来，但这些骨头仍可提供丰富的研究机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1629`

### English

Some sort of organic starship crashed on this world more than three billion years ago. Though only its ancient bones remain, the site offers a wealth of research opportunities.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1734`

## abandoned_cities

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aabandoned_cities&lang=en)

### 中文

遗弃之城

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1630`

### English

Abandoned Cities

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1735`

## abandoned_cities_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aabandoned_cities_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星球的原始居民已经神秘消失，留下他们几乎完好无缺的城市和基础设施。这将使它更容易发展成为新的殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1631`

### English

The original inhabitants of this world have mysteriously vanished, leaving behind their cities and infrastructure almost intact. This will make it much easier for a potential colony to develop.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1736`

## subterranean_ocean

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubterranean_ocean&lang=en)

### 中文

地下海洋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1632`

### English

Subterranean Ocean

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1737`

## subterranean_ocean_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubterranean_ocean_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗星球的冰质幔层下有一大片海洋，海底热泉为这片海洋提供着热量。深海中有大量生物存在，对这些有机物进行研究我们必能收获颇丰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1633`

### English

There is a large ocean heated by hydrothermal vents hidden beneath the ice mantle of this world. It is teeming with life, and much can be learned by studying these unique organisms.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1738`

## colony_enclaves

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_enclaves&lang=en)

### 中文

加固的飞地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1634`

### English

Fortified Enclaves

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1739`

## colony_enclaves_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony_enclaves_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星球上殖民者的活动范围将会被限制在指定的强化飞地中，而余下的土地将会让当地的石器时代土著和平地居住。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1635`

### English

The colony on this world is limited to several fortified enclaves. An indigenous Stone Age proto-civilization has been left in peace to develop independently on the remaining land.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1740`

## primitive_reservation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprimitive_reservation&lang=en)

### 中文

外星人保留地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1636`

### English

Alien Reservation

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1741`

## primitive_reservation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aprimitive_reservation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

外星殖民将这颗星球的一部分土地隔绝开来，留给本土的石器文明使用。这也在一定程度上限制了殖民者的土地和资源利用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1637`

### English

A part of this world has been turned into an enclosed reservation for an indigenous Stone Age proto-civilization. This has somewhat limited the land and resources available to colonists.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1742`

## starvation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astarvation&lang=en)

饥荒

### English

地底文明科技供应

### English

## subterranean_tech_transation_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asubterranean_tech_transation_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一部分的研究材料和资金将会专门用于研究在我们某一个殖民星上的地底文明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1640`

### English

A portion of our research materials and funds are earmarked for use by the subterranean civilization living inside one of our colonized planets.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1746`

## stellarite_high_temp_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astellarite_high_temp_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个浑身发光的巨兽出现在这个星系，它让这个星球的地表温度升高了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1642`

### English

A radiant monstrosity present in this system is increasing the surface temperature of the planet.

## stellarite_low_temp_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astellarite_low_temp_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个浑身发光的巨兽吞食了这个星系太阳的一部分，这使得星球的地表温度比往常要低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/modifiers_1_l_simp_chinese.yml:1644`

### English

A radiant monstrosity has devoured part of the star in this system, making the planetary bodies in orbit colder than usual.

Source: `localisation/english/modifiers_1_l_english.yml:1750`
